# D · part 2

1,309 plugins, delegate-gemini to developer-kit-project-management. [Back to the index](../README.md)

- **delegate-gemini**: Delegate a task to Google Gemini CLI to spread token usage and rate-limit pressure. ([geeknees/claude-plugin-opinionated](https://github.com/geeknees/claude-plugin-opinionated))
- **delegate-kit**: 7 specialist subagents (director, thinker, super-thinker, researcher, executer, simple-tasks, bug-hunter) + the /subagents playbook for when to use each. The director is the orchestrator: hand it an objective with /delegate-kit:run and it spawns the rest of the fleet, with no file access of its own. Run /delegate-kit:setup to compose them for your machine — works on built-ins alone, and wires in Serena, graphify, the Python defect taxonomy, or your own integrations when you have them. ([Thyra-AI/delegate-kit](https://github.com/Thyra-AI/delegate-kit))
- **delegate-local**: Run scoped tasks on a local opencode runtime as background subagents. Resumable event streaming, a warm shared runtime, and an MCP surface that also works under Codex CLI. ([tuomashatakka/delegate-local-plugin](https://github.com/tuomashatakka/delegate-local-plugin))
- **delegate-my-work**: Interview employees to find recurring work, map accessible AI and automation tools, and spec each loop with preferred and fallback routes. ([secondsky/claude-skills](https://github.com/secondsky/claude-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/delegate-my-work))
